# Review — 029-bootstrap-runtime-autowire

## Summary

Window from `4c6bf05b` (`base-source: last-review`, 029's last finished review), `review-owed: true`: 12 scope entries, each a file, no pattern, `framework/bootstrap/govern.md` among them as this run's uncommitted fix, with every other path the window changed excluded as another spec's commit and none deleted. Examined 11 of 12: `govern.md`, held byte-identical to `ductus.md`, was compared with `cmp` after each edit, not read. The five commits that bring paths into this scope were read for what they changed: `258c1c31`, `f24c55c6` and `cd06a227`, 022's task 18 corrections in `framework/bootstrap/ductus.md` and 029's `spec.md` (one CLI rule per subcommand a step runs, Antigravity's seed carrying none, the seed retiring an earlier seed's entries); `6aef0472`'s CLI form of `merge-permissions` in `framework/bootstrap/configure/claude.md`, which the bootstrap and configure files keep since `bbff7a53`; and `10302897`'s corrections for 079's retirement of State C and the markdown-only path in 029's `spec.md`, `plan.md` and scenario. Each matches `ductus.md` as it stands. The scenario `state-a-deterministic-path-forcing`, a durable contract the window changed, was read whole and each concrete claim checked against `ductus.md`. `specs/inbox.md` counts on 015's review's reading in this session. The five shared paths, `AGENTS.md`, the three runtime workflows and `runtime/Cargo.lock`, were read by 015's review in this session from `5bbc1887`, an older base than this window's, and are counted on that reading (SEC-DEPS-001, BE-DEPS-001, BE-DEPS-002). Of the 12 rule files, the passes applied SEC-DEPS-001, the BE-DEPS, QUAL and CFG rules: no other rule's subject is in these files. No finding. Two observations, fixed by the rule. `ductus.md` called the archive multi-hundred-KB in its pre-flight paragraph and pin step and its extract a few hundred KB in Cleanup, where `ductus-v0.59.11`'s archive is 3,523,152 bytes and its extract 11,927,790 (fetched from codeload and summed with `tar -tzv` on 2026-11-09, as `e188dff4` records for 015); each now says multi-megabyte or several megabytes, naming no version so the prose does not go stale at the next release, and `govern.md` follows. The scenario's reinforcement sentence called the `curl`/`tar` shown the fallback spec; it now says they specify what those primitives do, not commands to execute, as its corrected Behavior bullet and the State A reminder of `ductus.md`'s §File Fetching do. The six stored decisions name files outside this scope and are retained. No real adopter bootstrap ran for the `ductus.md` edit: it changes three size descriptors in prose and no step, input or command; `cargo test --release` passed over it, 2,804 tests. Fix rounds: round 1, from `start-fix-rounds`' base `1d42f36b`, held the three edited files and was re-read by an independent agent, which found one SHOULD in this run's own fix: the scenario edit had also moved the reminder to the Archive fetch and extract section and called §File Fetching its name when it shipped, but `## File Fetching` still stands (`ductus.md` line 781) with the reminder in its preamble (787), above `### Archive fetch and extract` (789). Its prescribed edit, restoring §File Fetching, was applied alone and `check-prescriptions` matched it with nothing open (round 2, diff-checked); the round from that snapshot was empty. The re-read found the three size edits accurate and `govern.md` mirroring `ductus.md`.
